GPU column-parallel exact version could get maybe 10-50 GFLOPS with O(n^3) traffic. For n=512 baseline 29ms; GPU exact version with per-column launches maybe ~2-5ms → 10x. But we want maximum.\n\nLet me first build an experimental exact column algorithm and test bitwise match. Not needed.\n\nActually for small n=512, launches dominate: 512*~4us=2ms, baseline 29ms so still ~14x.\n\nBut we can reduce launch overhead by using a persistent kernel with grid-wide sync via cooperative groups. No.\n\nAlternative: one kernel launch total, using spin-wait flags per column without cooperative launch (blocks may not all be resident -> deadlock if some blocks spin waiting while others not scheduled). Compute at n=2048 with this algorithm limited.\n\nHmm, can we improve algorithm while preserving per-element summation order? The fundamental arithmetic for each element is a dot product of length j. We can parallelize within dot product only if we preserve sequential order - no, a single thread must do it sequentially to match order. But we can use a blocked algorithm with higher arithmetic intensity while still computing each element's sum in original order? Storage: For output element A[i][j], want sum over k=0..j-1 of A[i][k]*A[j][k] in order. We could compute partial dot products into temporary arrays per element but order fixed. To improve data reuse, process a tile of (i,j) outputs together with sequential k, using shared memory tiles of A[i][k] and A[j][k]. That's a tiled \"left-looking\" update: for column block jb..jb+B-1 and row block ib..ib+B-1, accumulate over k in order. But the original order is k=0..j-1, and if we tile k, the sum for element (i,j) accumulates sequentially across k-tiles in order. Within each k-tile, each thread continues its sum sequentially. That preserves order! Great.\n\nSo we can implement a blocked/tiled version of the original left-looking algorithm:\n- Process diagonal blocks of columns in order: for a block of columns J = [j0, j0+B), we need to first compute the diagonal block L[j0:j0+B, j0:j0+B] which depends on previous columns k<j0 and also on columns within the block (column j depends on k in [j0,j)). Then update below-diagonal rows i >= j0+B for each column j in the block, summing over k=0..j-1.\n- For the below-diagonal block update, A[i][j] = (A_orig_remaining[i][j] - accumulated) / L[j][j], where accumulated sum over all k<j in order. We can do one GEMM-like pass over k=0..j0-1 first (tiles), then within-block k=j0..j-1.\n\nIf we do accumulation over k in increasing order within a single kernel per output tile, storing partial sums in registers/shared, we preserve exact order and get data reuse:\n- Each thread block computes a BxB tile of outputs for columns [j0, j0+B) and rows [ib, ib+B), with each thread computing one output element's dot product sequentially over k=0..j-1, loading A[i][k] and A[j][k] via shared memory tiles. Use shared memory: load B rows of A (rows i in block) for k tile, and B rows of A for columns j in block (rows j). Actually the second factor is A[j][k] for j in column block (rows j0..j0+B-1) and k; that's a B-wide set of rows. And first factor A[i][k] for i in row block. Both tiles loaded to shared memory per k-tile. Compute outer-product-like accumulation in registers: each thread accumulates its own dot product in order. Arithmetic intensity: for each k element, load 2B values from global, do B^2 FMAs. With B=32, 64 loads per 1024 FMAs = 16 FMA/load, good. Shared memory bandwidth: each thread reads 2 values per FMA, 2*8 bytes per 2 flops. Shared bandwidth is huge but this is still 8 bytes/flop vs tensor cores etc. FP throughput limited by shared memory: RTX 3090 shared bandwidth ~ 128 bytes/cycle/SM? At 1.7GHz*82 = ~17 TB/s; 8 bytes/flop → 2 TFLOP/s. Meh. Actually threads within a warp with same j and different i read same A[j][k] (broadcast, free) and different A[i][k] (coalesced). Let's think per thread per k does 1 FMA reading 2 shared values = 16 bytes. With broadcast, the j-value is shared among warp (same j? if thread layout: threadIdx.x = i, threadIdx.y = j, then within a warp j fixed → broadcast). So 8 bytes/thread/FMA. Right, diagonal block work is small if B small. Fine.\n\nBut wait, original algorithm's work for each column j includes computing B entries? No—within panel, entries below the diagonal within the panel (i,j with jb<=j<i<jb+B) are also computed as off-diagonal in the panel, and they need dot products length j. There are B^2/2 of these per panel, total nB/2 entries, work n^2B/4. Same as diagonal. So panel factorization (triangle of B columns) work O(n^2 B). Below-diagonal updates (i >= jb+B, j in panel) work: B*(n-jb) entries * j ≈ B*n^2/2 per... sum over panels ~ n^3/3 main work. Good.\n\nSo algorithm:\nFor each panel jb:\n  a. This preserves order across tiles if tiles processed increasing. Within a k-tile, loop k increasing. GCC vectorized the dot-product reduction using AVX (vmulpd/vaddsd sequence) with a specific summation order: it computes products in vector registers then adds lanes sequentially: sum += p0; sum += p1; sum += p2; sum += p3 for each group of 4. That's exactly sequential order! Look: vmovupd ymm4 (4 doubles), vmulpd -> ymm2; then vunpckhpd gets p1, vextractf128 gets p2,p3; adds: xmm0 += p0 (xmm2 low), += p1 (xmm3), += p2 (xmm1 low), += p3 (xmm1 high). So order is p0,p1,p2,p3 sequentially. That is the same order as scalar! But products are computed with vmulpd (separate multiply), then added with vaddsd. So it's mul+add in order. The tail uses vfmadd231sd — i.e., the leftover 1-3 elements use fused FMA! Interesting: so the CPU reference uses a mix: main vectorized part uses separate mul+add in sequential order; tail (last up to 3 k values) uses FMA. That explains why neither pure gpu_fma nor pure gpu_sep matches cpu_expr, and why cpu_expr != cpu_fma.\n\nSo to bitwise-match baseline, we must replicate: for each dot product, process k in groups of 4 using separate mul+add (4 sequential adds), and the last (j % 4) elements using FMA. Good—supports exact hash comparison.\n\nLet's now implement.\n\nKernel A (old sums) details:\n```\ntemplate<int TI>\n__global__ void panelOldSumKernel(double* __restrict__ S, const double* __restrict__ A,\n                                  int n, int jb, int B) {\n    // blockIdx.x -> row tile index; blockIdx.y -> col tile (only 1 since CT=B)\n    int row0 = jb + blockIdx.x * TI;\n    int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    int group = tid / TI;    // 0..7 (col group), if blockDim = 8*TI\n    int lane  = tid % TI;    // row within tile\n    int i = row0 + lane;\n    int j0 = jb + group*4;   // 4 columns\n    // accumulators for 4 cols\n    double acc0=0,acc1=0,acc2=0,acc3=0;\n    // shared tiles\n    extern __shared__ double smem[];\n    double* sA = smem;               // TI*K\n    double* sB = smem + TI*K;        // B*K\n    int K = 32;\n    for (int kt=0; kt<jb; kt+=K) {\n        int kkmax = min(K, jb-kt);\n        // load sA: TI*kkmax; using all threads\n        for (int idx=tid; idx<TI*kkmax; idx+=blockDim.x) {\n            int r = idx / kkmax, kk = idx % kkmax;\n            int ii = row0 + r;\n            sA[r*K + kk] = (ii<n) ? A[(size_t)ii*n + kt+kk] : 0.0;\n        }\n        // load sB: B*kkmax\n        for (int idx=tid; idx<B*kkmax; idx+=blockDim.x) {\n            int r = idx / kkmax, kk = idx % kkmax;\n            int jj = jb + r;\n            // jj<n always for r<B within panel; but last panel B may <32. Pass Bl.\n            sB[r*K + kk] = (jj<n) ? A[(size_t)jj*n + kt+kk] : 0.0;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n        // Only thread lanes with valid i (i<n) and j<=i? Compute all; write guarded later.\n        for (int kk=0; kk<kkmax; ++kk) {\n            double av = sA[lane*K + kk];\n            double b0 = sB[(group*4+0)*K + kk];\n            ... \n            acc0 = __dadd_rn(acc0, __dmul_rn(av,b0)); ...\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n    // store S\n    if (i<n) {\n        int si = (i-jb)*B;\n        int jj = group*4;\n        // only store columns j<=i, but storing all is fine (unused for j>i). But for band rows upper j>i: S index valid; no OOB.\n        S[si + jj+0] = acc0; ... (guard j0+3 < jb+B and j0+x < n)\n    }\n}\n```\nWait: groups mapping: group*4 corresponds to columns jb+group*4. For B=32, groups 0..7 → cols jb..jb+31. Good. Each block has 8*TI threads, one thread per (row lane, group). For TI<32, groups=8 still, threads=8*TI. Shared memory bank conflicts: 32 banks × 4 bytes. Reading stride 32 doubles (256 bytes) means all lanes hit same bank group? For double, banks... This is a classic transpose issue. Better store sA transposed as sA[kk][lane] so a fixed kk across lanes is contiguous. Loading from global: A[ii*n + k] with ii=row, k contiguous. The CUDA implementation keeps exactly the same floating point\\n// semantics: for every output element the partial sums are accumulated over\\n// k = 0 .. j-1 in increasing order, using a multiply followed by an add for\\n// the first (j & ~3) terms and a fused multiply-add for the remaining tail.\\n// This reproduces the reference result bit for bit.\\n//\\n// The work is organized in panels of PANEL columns:\\n//   1) oldSumKernel   : S[i][j] = sum_{k < jb} L[i][k] * L[j][k]  (tiled, bulk)\\n//   2) bandKernel     : factor the PANEL x PANEL diagonal block sequentially\\n//   3) trailingKernel : finish all rows below the panel (each block owns a\\n//                       set of rows and walks the panel columns in order)\\n// Column panels are processed from left to right, exactly like the reference.\\n\\nnamespace {\\n\\nconstexpr int PANEL    = 32;   // panel width (multiple of 4)\\nconstexpr int SSTRIDE  = 32;   // leading dimension used for the S buffer\\nconstexpr int KTILE    = 32;   // k tile used by the tiled old-sum kernel\\nconstexpr int BAND_THREADS = 256;\\nconstexpr int TRAIL_ROWS   = 128;\\n\\n__device__ __forceinline__ double d_mul_add(double a, double b, double c) {\\n    return __dadd_rn(__dmul_rn(a, b), c);\\n}\\n\\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n// 1) Old-column partial sums for the whole panel (bulk of the computation).\\n//    Grid: one block per row tile.  Each block covers PANEL columns.\\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\ntemplate <int TI>\\n__global__ void oldSumKernel(double* __restrict__ S,\\n                             const double* __restrict__ A,\\n                             int n, int jb) {\\n    const int tid   = threadIdx.x;\\n    const int group = tid / TI;          // which group of 4 panel columns\\n    const int lane  = tid - group * TI;  // row inside the tile\\n    const int row0  = jb + blockIdx.x * TI;\\n    const int i     = row0 + lane;\\n\\n    const int col0 = jb + group * 4;\\n\\n    extern __shared__ double smem[];\\n    double* sA = smem;                       // [KTILE][TI+1]\\n    double* sB = smem + KTILE * (TI + 1);    // [KTILE][PANEL+1]\\n\\n    double a0 = 0.0, a1 = 0.0, a2 = 0.0, a3 = 0.0;\\n\\n    for (int kt = 0; kt < jb; kt += KTILE) {\\n        const int kkmax = min(KTILE, jb - kt);\\n\\n        for (int idx = tid; idx < TI * kkmax; idx += blockDim.x) {\\n            const int r  = idx / kkmax;\\n            const int kk = idx - r * kkmax;\\n            const int ii = row0 + r;\\n            sA[kk * (TI + 1) + r] =\\n                (ii < n) ? A[(size_t)ii * n + (size_t)(kt + kk)] : 0.0;\\n        }\\n        for (int idx = tid; idx < PANEL * kkmax; idx += blockDim.x) {\\n            const int r  = idx / kkmax;\\n            const int kk = idx - r * kkmax;\\n            const int jj = jb + r;\\n            sB[kk * (PANEL + 1) + r] =\\n                (jj < n) ? A[(size_t)jj * n + (size_t)(kt + kk)] : 0.0;\\n        }\\n        __syncthreads();\\n\\n        for (int kk = 0; kk < kkmax; ++kk) {\\n            const double av = sA[kk * (TI + 1) + lane];\\n            const double* b = sB + kk * (PANEL + 1) + group * 4;\\n            a0 = d_mul_add(av, b[0], a0);\\n            a1 = d_mul_add(av, b[1], a1);\\n            a2 = d_mul_add(av, b[2], a2);\\n            a3 = d_mul_add(av, b[3], a3);\\n        }\\n        __syncthreads();\\n    }\\n\\n    if (i < n) {\\n        double* s = S + (size_t)(i - jb) * SSTRIDE + col0 - jb;\\n        s[0] = a0;\\n        s[1] = a1;\\n        s[2] = a2;\\n        s[3] = a3;\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n// 2) Factor the diagonal band of the panel.\\n//    Single block, columns are processed one after the other.\\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n__global__ void bandKernel(const double* __restrict__ S,\\n                           double* __restrict__ A,\\n                           int n, int jb, int bl, int* __restrict__ errj) {\\n    extern __shared__ double band[]; {"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1792474047570,"sessionID":"ses_ea8443189ffeseWOeG8UQ2P9FX","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"edit","callID":"call_00_m27mcdckr7tedto88cwwj70u","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/home/llmtest/evals/20261019-134042/cholesky_deepseek-v4.1-flash-oc-high_cuda_r2/cholesky/cholesky.cpp","oldString":"// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)\n// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // A is stored in row-major order\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            \n            if (i == j) {\n                // Diagonal element\n                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {\n                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];\n                }\n                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;\n                if (val <= 0.0) {\n                    // Matrix is not positive definite\n                    printf(\"Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\\n\", j);\n                    return false;\n                }\n                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);\n            } else {\n                // Off-diagonal element\n                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {\n                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];\n                }\n                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // Zero out upper triangular part\n        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}","newString":"// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// CUDA Cholesky decomposition: A = L * L^T, L lower triangular.\n//\n// The reference is a sequential, unblocked, left-looking algorithm.  The GPU\n// implementation keeps the exact same floating-point semantics: for every\n// output element the partial sums are accumulated over k = 0 .. j-1 in\n// increasing order, using a separate multiply + add for the first (j & ~3)\n// terms and a fused multiply-add for the remaining tail.  This reproduces the\n// reference result bit for bit.\n//\n// The columns are processed in panels of PANEL columns:\n//   1) oldSumKernel   : S[i][j] = sum_{k < jb} L[i][k] * L[j][k]  (bulk work)\n//   2) bandKernel     : factor the PANEL x PANEL diagonal block sequentially\n//   3) trailingKernel : finish all rows below the panel; every block owns a\n//                       set of rows and walks the panel columns in order\n// Panels are processed from left to right, exactly like the reference.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nnamespace {\n\nconstexpr int PANEL        = 32;   // panel width (multiple of 4)\nconstexpr int SSTRIDE      = 32;   // leading dimension of the S buffer\nconstexpr int KTILE        = 32;   // k tile of the tiled old-sum kernel\nconstexpr int BAND_THREADS = 256;\nconstexpr int TRAIL_ROWS   = 128;\n\n__device__ __forceinline__ double d_mul_add(double a, double b, double c) {\n    return __dadd_rn(__dmul_rn(a, b), c);\n}\n\n// 1) Old-column partial sums for a whole panel (the bulk of the work).\ntemplate <int TI>\n__global__ void oldSumKernel(double* __restrict__ S,\n                             const double* __restrict__ A,\n                             int n, int jb) {\n    const int tid   = threadIdx.x;\n    const int group = tid / TI;\n    const int lane  = tid - group * TI;\n    const int row0  = jb + blockIdx.x * TI;\n    const int i     = row0 + lane;\n    const int col0  = jb + group * 4;\n\n    extern __shared__ double smem[];\n    double* sA = smem;                       // [KTILE][TI+1]\n    double* sB = smem + KTILE * (TI + 1);    // [KTILE][PANEL+1]\n\n    double a0 = 0.0, a1 = 0.0, a2 = 0.0, a3 = 0.0;\n\n    for (int kt = 0; kt < jb; kt += KTILE) {\n        const int kkmax = min(KTILE, jb - kt);\n\n        for (int idx = tid; idx < TI * kkmax; idx += blockDim.x) {\n            const int r  = idx / kkmax;\n            const int kk = idx - r * kkmax;\n            const int ii = row0 + r;\n            sA[kk * (TI + 1) + r] =\n                (ii < n) ? A[(size_t)ii * n + (size_t)(kt + kk)] : 0.0;\n        }\n        for (int idx = tid; idx < PANEL * kkmax; idx += blockDim.x) {\n            const int r  = idx / kkmax;\n            const int kk = idx - r * kkmax;\n            const int jj = jb + r;\n            sB[kk * (PANEL + 1) + r] =\n                (jj < n) ? A[(size_t)jj * n + (size_t)(kt + kk)] : 0.0;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        for (int kk = 0; kk < kkmax; ++kk) {\n            const double av = sA[kk * (TI + 1) + lane];\n            const double* b = sB + kk * (PANEL + 1) + group * 4;\n            a0 = d_mul_add(av, b[0], a0);\n            a1 = d_mul_add(av, b[1], a1);\n            a2 = d_mul_add(av, b[2], a2);\n            a3 = d_mul_add(av, b[3], a3);\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (i < n) {\n        double* s = S + (size_t)(i - jb) * SSTRIDE + (col0 - jb);\n        s[0] = a0;\n        s[1] = a1;\n        s[2] = a2;\n        s[3] = a3;\n    }\n}\n\n// 2) Factor the diagonal band of the panel, column by column.\n__global__ void bandKernel(const double* __restrict__ S,\n                           double* __restrict__ A,\n                           int n, int jb, int bl, int* __restrict__ errj) {\n    extern __shared__ double band[];  // [bl][bl]\n\n    for (int jj = 0; jj < bl; ++jj) {\n        const int j   = jb + jj;\n        const int lim = j & ~3;\n\n        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {\n            double acc = S[(size_t)jj * SSTRIDE + jj];\n            for (int k = jb; k < lim; ++k) {\n                const double a = band[jj * bl + (k - jb)];\n                acc = d_mul_add(a, a, acc);\n            }\n            for (int k = lim; k < j; ++k) {\n                const double a = band[jj * bl + (k - jb)];\n                acc = fma(a, a, acc);\n            }\n            double val = A[(size_t)j * n + j] - acc;\n            if (val <= 0.0) {\n                atomicMin(errj, j + 1);\n                val = 0.0;\n            }\n            const double d = sqrt(val);\n            A[(size_t)j * n + j] = d;\n            band[jj * bl + jj] = d;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const double dj = band[jj * bl + jj];\n        for (int ii = jj + 1 + threadIdx.x; ii < bl; ii += blockDim.x) {\n            const int i = jb + ii;\n            double acc = S[(size_t)ii * SSTRIDE + jj];\n            for (int k = jb; k < lim; ++k) {\n                acc = d_mul_add(band[ii * bl + (k - jb)],\n                                band[jj * bl + (k - jb)], acc);\n            }\n            for (int k = lim; k < j; ++k) {\n                acc = fma(band[ii * bl + (k - jb)],\n                          band[jj * bl + (k - jb)], acc);\n            }\n            const double v = (A[(size_t)i * n + j] - acc) / dj;\n            A[(size_t)i * n + j] = v;\n            band[ii * bl + jj] = v;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n}\n\n// 3) Finalize all rows below the panel.\n__global__ void trailingKernel(const double* __restrict__ S,\n                               double* __restrict__ A,\n                               int n, int jb, int bl) {\n    extern __shared__ double srow[];  // [TRAIL_ROWS][PANEL]\n    const int lane = threadIdx.x;\n    const int i    = jb + bl + blockIdx.x * TRAIL_ROWS + lane;\n\n    if (i < n) {\n        double* r = srow + (size_t)lane * PANEL;\n        for (int jj = 0; jj < bl; ++jj) {\n            const int j   = jb + jj;\n            const int lim = j & ~3;\n            double acc = S[(size_t)(i - jb) * SSTRIDE + jj];\n            for (int k = jb; k < lim; ++k) {\n                acc = d_mul_add(r[k - jb], A[(size_t)j * n + k], acc);\n            }\n            for (int k = lim; k < j; ++k) {\n                acc = fma(r[k - jb], A[(size_t)j * n + k], acc);\n            }\n            r[jj] = (A[(size_t)i * n + j] - acc) / A[(size_t)j * n + j];\n        }\n        for (int jj = 0; jj < bl; ++jj) {\n            A[(size_t)i * n + (jb + jj)] = r[jj];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n__global__ void zeroUpperKernel(double* __restrict__ A, int n) {\n    const size_t idx = (size_t)blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (idx >= (size_t)n * n) return;\n    const int i = (int)(idx / n);\n    const int j = (int)(idx % n);\n    if (j > i) A[idx] = 0.0;\n}\n\ntemplate <int TI>\nvoid launchOldSumTI(double* S, const double* A, int n, int jb, int rows) {\n    const int blocks = (rows + TI - 1) / TI;\n    const size_t sh = (size_t)KTILE * (TI + 1) +\n                      (size_t)KTILE * (PANEL + 1);\n    oldSumKernel<TI><<<blocks, 8 * TI, sh * sizeof(double)>>>(S, A, n, jb);\n}\n\nvoid launchOldSum(double* S, const double* A, int n, int jb, int rows,\n                  int sms) {\n    if (rows >= 32 * sms) {\n        launchOldSumTI<32>(S, A, n, jb, rows);\n    } else if (rows >= 16 * sms) {\n        launchOldSumTI<16>(S, A, n, jb, rows);\n    } else if (rows >= 8 * sms) {\n        launchOldSumTI<8>(S, A, n, jb, rows);\n    } else {\n        launchOldSumTI<4>(S, A, n, jb, rows);\n    }\n}\n\n}  // namespace\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    if (n == 0) return true;\n    if (n > (size_t)2147483647) {\n        printf(\"Matrix size too large\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    const int ni = (int)n;\n\n    double* dA = nullptr;\n    double* dS = nullptr;\n    int* d_err = nullptr;\n    if (cudaMalloc(&dA, (size_t)n * n * sizeof(double)) != cudaSuccess ||\n        cudaMalloc(&dS, (size_t)n * PANEL * sizeof(double)) != cudaSuccess ||\n        cudaMalloc(&d_err, sizeof(int)) != cudaSuccess) {\n        printf(\"CUDA allocation failed\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    cudaMemset(d_err, 0, sizeof(int));\n    if (cudaMemcpy(dA, A.data(), (size_t)n * n * sizeof(double),\n                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice) != cudaSuccess) {\n        printf(\"CUDA copy to device failed\\n\");\n        cudaFree(dA);\n        cudaFree(dS);\n        cudaFree(d_err);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    int sms = 1;\n    cudaDeviceProp prop;\n    if (cudaGetDeviceProperties(&prop, 0) == cudaSuccess) {\n        sms = prop.multiProcessorCount > 0 ? prop.multiProcessorCount : 1;\n    }\n\n    for (int jb = 0; jb < ni; jb += PANEL) {\n        const int bl = min(PANEL, ni - jb);\n        const int rows = ni - jb;\n\n        launchOldSum(dS, dA, ni, jb, rows, sms);\n\n        const size_t bandBytes = (size_t)bl * bl * sizeof(double);\n        bandKernel<<<1, BAND_THREADS, bandBytes>>>(dS, dA, ni, jb, bl, d_err);\n\n        const int trailRows = ni - jb - bl;\n        if (trailRows > 0) {\n            const int blocks = (trailRows + TRAIL_ROWS - 1) / TRAIL_ROWS;\n            const size_t srBytes = (size_t)TRAIL_ROWS * PANEL * sizeof(double);\n            trailingKernel<<<blocks, TRAIL_ROWS, srBytes>>>(dS, dA, ni, jb, bl);\n        }\n    }\n\n    {\n        const size_t total = (size_t)n * n;\n        const int threads = 256;\n        const long long blocks =\n            (long long)((total + (size_t)threads - 1) / (size_t)threads);\n        zeroUpperKernel<<<(unsigned int)blocks, threads>>>(dA, ni);\n    }\n\n    cudaError_t err = cudaDeviceSynchronize();\n    if (err != cudaSuccess) {\n        printf(\"CUDA error: %s\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err));\n        cudaFree(dA);\n        cudaFree(dS);\n        cudaFree(d_err);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    int errj = 0;\n    cudaMemcpy(&errj, d_err, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost);\n    cudaMemcpy(A.data(), dA, (size_t)n * n * sizeof(double),\n               cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost);\n\n    cudaFree(dA);\n    cudaFree(dS);\n    cudaFree(d_err);\n\n    if (errj > 0) {\n        printf(\"Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %d\\n\",\n               errj - Loads then coalesced better. But rows per thread 2 means two accumulators? Each thread processes rows i and i+TRAIL_ROWS? Wait blockDim=32, rows per block=TRAIL_ROWS. Let thread t handle rows base+t and base+t+32 (if TRAIL_ROWS=64). Each row has separate sS row segment; store sS[TRAIL_ROWS][bl]. Then loop two rows per thread. More ILP. For n=2048 64 blocks → 64 SMs with 1 block each (since 64<82). Fine. Use TRAIL_ROWS=32, blockDim=32.\n\nLet's go with TRAIL_ROWS=32, blockDim=TRAIL_ROWS. Since each warp instruction is 32-wide, but FP64 unit processes 2/cycle → a warp instruction takes 16 cycles. With 4 independent ops issued back-to-back, the FP64 pipe issues one warp instruction every 16 cycles… Actually one warp's instruction uses all 2 FP64 lanes for 16 cycles (32 lanes / 2 per cycle). So a single warp can saturate the FP64 pipe only if it has enough independent instructions to issue back-to-back. 4 accumulators → 8 instructions (mul,add ×4) per k. Each mul/add warp instruction takes 16 cycles in the pipe but can be pipelined; a single warp can issue every cycle? S[(size_t)(i - jb) * SSTRIDE + jj] : 0.0;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    const int i = base + lane;\n    if (i < n) {\n        double* r = srow + (size_t)lane * bl;\n        for (int jj = 0; jj < bl; ++jj) {\n            const int j   = jb + jj;\n            const int lim = j & ~3;\n            double acc = r[jj];\n            for (int k = jb; k < lim; ++k) {\n                acc = d_mul_add(r[k - jb], sband[jj * bl + (k - jb)], acc);\n            }\n            for (int k = lim; k < j; ++k) {\n                acc = fma(r[k - jb], sband[jj * bl + (k - jb)], acc);\n            }\n            r[jj] = (A[(size_t)i * n + j] - acc) / sband[jj * bl + jj];\n        }\n        for (int jj = 0; jj < bl; ++jj) {\n            A[(size_t)i * n + (jb + jj)] = r[jj];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n__global__ void zeroUpperKernel(double* __restrict__ A, int n) {\n    const size_t idx = (size_t)blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (idx >= (size_t)n * n) return;\n    const int i = (int)(idx / n);\n    const int j = (int)(idx % n);\n    if (j > i) A[idx] = 0.0;\n}\n\ntemplate <int TI>\nvoid launchOldSumTI(double* S, const double* A, int n, int jb, int rows) {\n    const int blocks = (rows + TI - 1) / TI;\n    const size_t sh = (size_t)KTILE * (TI + 1) +\n                      (size_t)KTILE * (PANEL + 1);\n    oldSumKernel<TI><<<blocks, (PANEL / OLDS_COLS) * TI, sh * sizeof(double)>>>(S, A, n, jb);\n}\n\nvoid launchOldSum(double* S, const double* A, int n, int jb, int rows,\n                  int sms) {\n    if (rows >= 32 * sms) {\n        launchOldSumTI<32>(S, A, n, jb, rows);\n    } else if (rows >= 16 * sms) {\n        launchOldSumTI<16>(S, A, n, jb, rows);\n    } else if (rows >= 8 * sms) {\n        launchOldSumTI<8>(S, A, n, jb, rows);\n    } else {\n        launchOldSumTI<4>(S, A, n, jb, rows);\n    }\n}\n\n}  // namespace\n\nstatic bool g_pinned_a = false;\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    if (n == 0) return true;\n    if (n > (size_t)2147483647) {\n        printf(\"Matrix size too large\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    const int ni = (int)n;\n\n    double* dA = nullptr;\n    double* dS = nullptr;\n    int* d_err = nullptr;\n    if (cudaMalloc(&dA, (size_t)n * n * sizeof(double)) != cudaSuccess ||\n        cudaMalloc(&dS, (size_t)n * PANEL * sizeof(double)) != cudaSuccess ||\n        cudaMalloc(&d_err, sizeof(int)) != cudaSuccess) {\n        printf(\"CUDA allocation failed\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    cudaMemset(d_err, 0, sizeof(int));\n    if (cudaMemcpyAsync(dA, A.data(), (size_t)n * n * sizeof(double),\n                        cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, 0) != cudaSuccess) {\n        printf(\"CUDA copy to device failed\\n\");\n        cudaFree(dA);\n        cudaFree(dS);\n        cudaFree(d_err);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    int sms = 1;\n    cudaDeviceProp prop;\n    if (cudaGetDeviceProperties(&prop, 0) == cudaSuccess) {\n        sms = prop.multiProcessorCount > 0 ? prop.multiProcessorCount : 1;\n    }\n\n    for (int jb = 0; jb < ni; jb += PANEL) {\n        const int bl = min(PANEL, ni - jb);\n        const int rows = ni - jb;\n\n        launchOldSum(dS, dA, ni, jb, rows, sms);\n\n        const size_t bandBytes = (size_t)3 * bl * bl * sizeof(double);\n        bandKernel<<<1, BAND_THREADS, bandBytes>>>(dS, dA, ni, jb, bl, d_err);\n\n        const int trailRows = ni - jb - bl;\n        if (trailRows > 0) {\n            const int blocks = (trailRows + TRAIL_ROWS - 1) / TRAIL_ROWS;\n            const size_t srBytes = (size_t)(TRAIL_ROWS * PANEL +\n                                            PANEL * PANEL) * sizeof(double);\n            trailingKernel<<<blocks, TRAIL_ROWS, srBytes>>>(dS, dA, ni, jb, bl);\n        }\n    }\n\n    {\n        const size_t total = (size_t)n * n;\n        const int threads = 256;\n        const long long blocks =\n            (long long)((total + (size_t)threads - 1) / (size_t)threads);\n        zeroUpperKernel<<<(unsigned int)blocks, threads>>>(dA, ni);\n    }\n\n    cudaError_t err = cudaDeviceSynchronize();\n    if (err != cudaSuccess) {\n        printf(\"CUDA error: %s\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err));\n        cudaFree(dA);\n        cudaFree(dS);\n        cudaFree(d_err);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    int errj = 0;\n    cudaMemcpy(&errj, d_err, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost);\n    cudaMemcpyAsync(A.data(), dA, (size_t)n * n * sizeof(double),\n                    cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, 0);\n    cudaStreamSynchronize(0);\n\n    cudaFree(dA);\n    cudaFree(dS);\n    cudaFree(d_err);\n\n    if (errj > 0) {\n        printf(\"Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %d\\n\",\n               errj -
